        bl = self.buildTree(grid, re - next_level, re, cs, ce - next_level, next_level)

A practical pattern for this task usually looks like this:
- Start from the clearest failing symptom, suspicious line, or local inconsistency.
- Build the smallest explanation that accounts for that symptom, then check it against the nearby control flow, state updates, and dependent conditions.
- Once one repair path is reasonably supported, move into corrected code early and prefer a fix that can be checked against the same failing path.
- After the edit, rerun that path or the most relevant edge case.
- Then do one small coupled-region scan: a directly connected guard, index update, return path, or call site that could still break the same fix.
- If the recheck is still off, refine the same local region before opening a distant new theory.
- Once the failing path and its directly coupled region look consistent, finalize the code.

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        level = len(grid)
        root = None
        if level >= 1:
            root = self.buildTree(grid1, 0, level, 0, level, level)

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        mid = level // 2
        topLeft = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)
        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

        if (topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and
                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):
            return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(topLeft.val, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)
```

Let's maybe produce final code:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        mid = level // 2
        topLeft = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)
        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

        if (topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and
                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):
            return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(topLeft.val, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)
```

Let's maybe consider if there is a more concise version:
```
class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        def dfs(r, c, size):
            if size == 1:
                return Node(grid[r][c] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)
            half = size // 2
            tl = dfs(r, c, half)
            tr = dfs(r, c + half, half)
            bl = dfs(r + half, c, half)
            br = dfs(r + half, c + half, half)
            if tl.isLeaf and tr.isLeaf and bl.isLeaf and br.isLeaf and tl.val == tr.val == bl.val == br.val:
                return Node(tl.val, True, None, None, None, None)
            return Node(tl.val, False, tl, tr, bl, br)
        return dfs(0, 0, len(grid))
```
This is simpler and avoids rs/re/cs/ce. But original uses buildTree. Should we output fixed original or a cleaner version? The task says "Fix the code and output only the fixed code." ```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        mid = level // 2
        topLeft = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)
        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

        if (topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and
                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):
            return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(topLeft.val, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)
```

Let's maybe decide final code exactly:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        mid = level // 2
        topLeft = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)
        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

        if (topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and
                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):
            return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(topLeft.val, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)
```

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        mid = level // 2
        topLeft = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)
        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

        if (topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and
                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):
            return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(topLeft.val, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)
```

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        mid = level // 2
        topLeft = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)
        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

        if (topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and
                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):
            return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        mid = level // 2
        topLeft = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)
        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

        if (topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and
                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):
            return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(topLeft.val, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)
```

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        mid = level // 2
        topLeft = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)
        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

        if (topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and
                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):
            return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(topLeft.val, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)
```

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

        topLeft = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)
        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

        if (topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and
                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):
            return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(topLeft.val, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        mid = level // 2
        topLeft = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)
        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

        if (topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and
                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):
            return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(topLeft.val, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)
```

Let's maybe decide final code exactly:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        mid = level // 2
        topLeft = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)
        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

        if (topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and
                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):
            return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(topLeft.val, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)
```

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        mid = level // 2
        topLeft = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)
        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

        if (topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and
                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):
            return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(topLeft.val, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)
```

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        mid = level // 2
        topLeft = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)
        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

        if (topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and
                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):
            return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(topLeft.val, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)
```

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        mid = level // 2
        topLeft = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)
        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

        if (topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and
                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):
            return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(topLeft.val, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)
```

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):

            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        mid = level // 2
        topLeft = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)
        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        mid = level // 2
        topLeft = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)
        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

        if (topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and
                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):
            return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(topLeft.val, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)
```

Let's maybe decide final code exactly:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        mid = level // 2
        topLeft = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)
        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

        if (topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and
                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):
            return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(topLeft.val, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)
```

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):

    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        mid = level // 2
        topLeft = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)
        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

        if (topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and
                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):
            return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(topLeft.val, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)
```

Let's maybe decide final code exactly:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        mid = level // 2
        topLeft = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)
        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

        if (topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and
                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):
            return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(topLeft.val, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)
```

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        mid = level // 2
        topLeft = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)
        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

        if (topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and
                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):
            return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(topLeft.val, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)
```

Let's maybe decide final code exactly:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        mid = level // 2
        topLeft = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)
        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

        if (topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and
                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):
            return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(topLeft.val, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)
```

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        mid = level // 2

        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        mid = level // 2
        topLeft = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)
        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

        if (topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and
                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):
            return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(topLeft.val, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)
```

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':

        if n == 0:

        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

        if level == 1:

        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def construct(self, grid: List[List[int]]) -> 'Node':
        n = len(grid)
        if n == 0:
            return None
        return self.buildTree(grid, 0, n, 0, n, n)

    def buildTree(self, grid, rs, re, cs, ce, level):
        if level == 1:
            return Node(grid[rs][cs] == 1, True, None, None, None, None)

        mid = level // 2
        topLeft = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        topRight = self.buildTree(grid, rs, re - mid, ce - mid, ce, mid)
        bottomLeft = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, cs, ce - mid, mid)
        bottomRight = self.buildTree(grid, re - mid, re, ce - mid, ce, mid)

        if (topLeft.isLeaf and topRight.isLeaf and bottomLeft.isLeaf and bottomRight.isLeaf and
                topLeft.val == topRight.val == bottomLeft.val == bottomRight.val):
            return Node(topLeft.val, True, None, None, None, None)

        return Node(topLeft.val, False, topLeft, topRight, bottomLeft, bottomRight)
